# Phase 2 deletion manifest

Status: steps 1–7 implemented. The machine-readable
[test disposition inventory](phase-2-test-disposition.json) is the per-case
record. Baseline `aa36477` is the parent of Step 1. No old driver/host adapter
remains.

## Inventory

The original 147 core, Remote and release test modules contain 742 literal title
entries. Full parameterized title patterns supplement those entries below; each
pattern covers all values in its original loop. These are source-case counts,
not executed test counts. Retained embedded tests do not establish native kernel
guarantees.

| Disposition | Source cases |
| ----------- | -----------: |
| Retained    |          434 |
| Replaced    |          229 |
| Deferred    |           17 |
| Deleted     |          138 |

## Dependent deletions

Step 6 also removed the old Remote benchmark execution/test, CLI remote-reload
cases, chatbot Remote setup, Remote sample 06 and packed ecosystem script.
Historical benchmark data remains data. Step 7 removes the old
integration-gateway release/probe fixture packages and replaces them with a
runnable public fixed-deployment sample. These paths cannot invoke the deleted
engine. Release storage/journal tests remain; kernel release integration is
Phase 5.
